class=\"solution solution-like born-hidden-knowl\"><summary class=\"knowl__link\"><span class=\"type\">Solution</span></summary><div class=\"solution solution-like knowl__content\"><div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-7-6-6-2-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">Si <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|f|\\)</span> admet un maximum en <span class=\"process-math\">\\(0\\text{,}\\)</span> alors pour tout <span class=\"process-math\">\\(r \\in [0, R[\\text{,}\\)</span> on a :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n|f(0)| = \\sup_{t \\in \\mathbb{R}} |f(r \\cos(t), r \\sin(t))|\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">Ainsi, <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|f|\\)</span> est constante sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(D(0, R)\\text{.}\\)</span> Comme <span class=\"process-math\">\\(f\\)</span> est analytique, cela implique que <span class=\"process-math\">\\(f\\)</span> est constante sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(D(0, R)\\text{.}\\)</span>\n</div>\n</div></div></details></div></article><article class=\"exercise exercise-like\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-7-6-7\"><h6 class=\"heading\">\n<span class=\"before\"> </span><span class=\"codenumber\">37</span><span class=\"after\"> </span><span class=\"space\"> </span>\n</h6>\n<div class=\"para\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-7-6-7-1-1\">Montrer le théorème de d’Alembert-Gauss : tout polynôme complexe non constant admet au moins une racine.</div>\n<div class=\"solutions\"><details id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-7-6-7-2\" class=\"solution solution-like born-hidden-knowl\"><summary class=\"knowl__link\"><span class=\"type\">Solution</span></summary><div class=\"solution solution-like knowl__content\"><div class=\"para\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-7-6-7-2-1\">Supposons par l’absurde qu’il existe un polynôme complexe non constant <span class=\"process-math\">\\(P\\)</span> qui ne s’annule pas sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathbb{C}\\text{.}\\)</span> Alors <span class=\"process-math\">\\(1/P\\)</span> est une fonction entière (analytique sur tout <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathbb{C}\\)</span>). Comme <span class=\"process-math\">\\(P\\)</span> est non constant, <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|P(z)| \\to +\\infty\\)</span> lorsque <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|z| \\to +\\infty\\text{,}\\)</span> donc <span class=\"process-math\">\\(1/P\\)</span> est bornée sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathbb{C}\\text{.}\\)</span> Par le théorème de Liouville, <span class=\"process-math\">\\(1/P\\)</span> est constante, ce qui implique que <span class=\"process-math\">\\(P\\)</span> est constant, ce qui est une contradiction. Ainsi, <span class=\"process-math\">\\(P\\)</span> admet au moins une racine.</div></div></details></div></article></section></section><section class=\"subsection\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8\"><h4 class=\"heading hide-type\">\n<span class=\"codenumber\">1.1.5</span><span class=\"type\">Sous-section</span><span class=\"title\">Résolution du problème de Dirichlet dans le disque unité de \\(\\mathbb{R}^2\\)</span>\n</h4>\n<section class=\"introduction\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-2\"><div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-2-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">Soit <span class=\"process-math\">\\(h\\)</span> une fonction de <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathbb{R}\\)</span> dans <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathbb{R}\\text{,}\\)</span> continue et <span class=\"process-math\">\\(2\\pi\\)</span>-périodique sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathbb{R}\\text{.}\\)</span> On cherche à résoudre le problème de Dirichlet sur le disque unité ; autrement dit, il s’agit de déterminer, s’il y en a, la ou les fonctions <span class=\"process-math\">\\(f\\)</span> définies et continues sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(D(0, 1)\\)</span> (disque fermé), de classe <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathcal{C}^2\\)</span> sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(D(0, 1)\\text{,}\\)</span> et telles que</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\begin{cases}\n\\Delta f = 0 \\text{ sur } D(0, 1) \\\\\n\\forall t \\in \\mathbb{R}, \\, f(\\cos(t), \\sin(t)) = h(t)\n\\end{cases}\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n</div></section><article class=\"exercise exercise-like\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-3\"><h5 class=\"heading\">\n<span class=\"before\"> </span><span class=\"codenumber\">38</span><span class=\"after\"> </span><span class=\"space\"> </span>\n</h5>\n<div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-3-1-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">Pour tout nombre complexe <span class=\"process-math\">\\(z\\)</span> tel que <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|z| \\lt 1\\text{,}\\)</span> on pose</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\ng(z) =\n\\frac{1}{2\\pi} \\int_0^{2\\pi} h(t) \\mathcal{P}(t, z) \\, dt\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">où</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\mathcal{P}(t,\nz) = \\operatorname{Re} \\left( \\frac{e^{it} + z}{e^{it} - z} \\right)\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">Montrer que la fonction <span class=\"process-math\">\\(z \\mapsto \\frac{e^{it} + z}{e^{it} - z}\\)</span> est développable en série entière pour <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|z| \\lt 1\\)</span> et calculer son développement en série entière. En déduire que la fonction <span class=\"process-math\">\\((x, y) \\mapsto g(x + i y)\\)</span> est une fonction harmonique sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(D(0, 1)\\text{.}\\)</span>\n</div>\n</div>\n<div class=\"solutions\"><details id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-3-2\" class=\"solution solution-like born-hidden-knowl\"><summary class=\"knowl__link\"><span class=\"type\">Solution</span></summary><div class=\"solution solution-like knowl__content\">\n<div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-3-2-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">Pour <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|z| \\lt 1\\text{,}\\)</span> on a :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\frac{e^{it} + z}{e^{it} - z} = \\frac{1 + z\ne^{-it}}{1 - z e^{-it}} = (1 + z e^{-it}) \\sum_{n=0}^{+\\infty} z^n e^{-i n t} =\n\\sum_{n=0}^{+\\infty} z^n e^{-i n t} + \\sum_{n=0}^{+\\infty} z^{n+1} e^{-i (n+1)\nt}\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">En regroupant les termes, on obtient :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\frac{e^{it} + z}{e^{it}\n- z}\n= 1 + 2 \\sum_{n=1}^{+\\infty} z^n e^{-i n t}\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">Ainsi, la fonction <span class=\"process-math\">\\(z\n\\mapsto\n\\frac{e^{it} + z}{e^{it} - z}\\)</span> est développable en série entière pour <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|z|\n\\lt 1\\text{.}\\)</span>\n</div>\n</div>\n<div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-3-2-2\">\n<div class=\"para\">En prenant la partie réelle, on a :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\mathcal{P}(t, z) = \\operatorname{Re}\n\\left( \\frac{e^{it} + z}{e^{it} - z} \\right) = 1 + 2 \\sum_{n=1}^{+\\infty}\n\\operatorname{Re}(z^n e^{-i n t})\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">La fonction <span class=\"process-math\">\\(g(z)\\)</span> est donc une combinaison linéaire de fonctions harmoniques (les parties réelles des monômes <span class=\"process-math\">\\(z^n\\)</span>), donc <span class=\"process-math\">\\(g\\)</span> est harmonique sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(D(0, 1)\\text{.}\\)</span>\n</div>\n</div>\n</div></details></div></article><article class=\"exercise exercise-like\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-4\"><h5 id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-4-1-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">Montrer que, pour tout nombre complexe <span class=\"process-math\">\\(z\\)</span> tel que <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|z| \\lt class=\"solutions\"><details id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-4-2\" class=\"solution solution-like born-hidden-knowl\"><summary class=\"knowl__link\"><span class=\"type\">Solution</span></summary><div class=\"solution solution-like knowl__content\"><div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-4-2-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">D’après le développement en série entière de <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathcal{P}(t, z)\\text{,}\\)</span> on a :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\mathcal{P}(t,\nz) = 1 + 2 \\sum_{n=1}^{+\\infty} process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\int_0^{2\\pi} e^{-i n t} \\, dt = 0\n\\quad \\text{pour } n \\geq 1\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">Ainsi, il reste :</div>\n<div class=\"exercise exercise-like\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-5\"><h5 class=\"heading\">\n<span class=\"before\"> </span><span class=\"codenumber\">40</span><span class=\"after\"> </span><span class=\"space\"> </span>\n</h5>\n<div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-5-1-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">Soit <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\varphi \\in \\mathbb{R}\\text{.}\\)</span> Montrer que, pour tout nombre complexe <span class=\"process-math\">\\(z\\)</span> tel que <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|z| \\lt 1\\text{,}\\)</span>\n</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-5-2\" class=\"solution solution-like born-hidden-knowl\"><summary class=\"knowl__link\"><span class=\"type\">Solution</span></summary><div class=\"solution solution-like knowl__content\"><div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-5-2-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">La fonction <span class=\"process-math\">\\(h\\)</span> est <span class=\"process-math\">\\(2\\pi\\)</span>-périodique, donc l’intégrale de <span class=\"process-math\">\\(h(t)\n\\mathcal{P}(t, z)\\)</span> sur un intervalle de longueur <span Ainsi :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\int_{\\varphi}^{\\varphi + 2\\pi} h(t)\n\\mathcal{P}(t, z) \\, dt = \\int_0^{2\\pi} h(t) \\mathcal{P}(t, z) \\, dt\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">Donc :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\ng(z)\n= \\frac{1}{2\\pi} \\int_{\\varphi}^{\\varphi + 2\\pi} h(t) \\mathcal{P}(t, z) \\, dt\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n</div></div></details></div></article><article class=\"exercise exercise-like\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-6\"><h5 class=\"heading\">\n<span class=\"before\"> </span><span class=\"codenumber\">41</span><span class=\"after\"> </span><span class=\"space\"> </span>\n</h5>\n<div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-6-1-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">Montrer que, pour tout <span class=\"process-math\">\\(r \\in [0, 1[\\)</span> et tous réels <span class=\"process-math\">\\(t\\)</span> et <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\theta\\)</span> ,</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\mathcal{P}(t, r e^{i \\theta}) = \\frac{1 - r^2}{1 - 2 r\n\\cos(t - \\theta) + r^2}\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n</div>\n<div class=\"solutions\"><details id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-6-2\" class=\"solution solution-like born-hidden-knowl\"><summary class=\"knowl__link\"><span class=\"type\">Solution</span></summary><div class=\"solution solution-like knowl__content\"><div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-6-2-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">En utilisant l’expression de <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathcal{P}(t, z)\\text{,}\\)</span> on a :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\mathcal{P}(t,\nr e^{i \\theta}) = \\operatorname{Re} \\left( \\frac{e^{it} + r e^{i \\theta}}{e^{it}\n- r e^{i \\theta}} \\right)\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">En multipliant le numérateur et le dénominateur par <span class=\"process-math\">\\(e^{-it}\\text{,}\\)</span> on obtient :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\mathcal{P}(t, r e^{i \\theta}) =\n\\operatorname{Re} \\left( \\frac{1 + r e^{i (\\theta - |z|^2}{|1 - z|^2}\\text{,}\\)</span> on trouve :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\mathcal{P}(t,\nr e^{i \\theta}) = \\frac{1 - r^2}{1 - 2 r \\cos(t - \\theta) + r^2}\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n</div></div></details></div></article><article class=\"exercise exercise-like\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-7\"><h5 class=\"heading\">\n<span class=\"before\"> </span><span class=\"codenumber\">42</span><span class=\"after\"> </span><span class=\"space\"> </span>\n</h5>\n<div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-7-1-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">Montrer que, pour tout <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\delta \\in [0, \\pi[\\)</span> et tout réel <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\varphi\\text{,}\\)</span>\n</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\int_{\\varphi\n+ \\delta}^{\\varphi + 2\\pi - \\delta} \\mathcal{P}(t, z) \\, dt \\to 0 \\quad\n\\text{lorsque } |z| \\to 1\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n</div>\n<div class=\"solutions\"><details id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-7-2\" class=\"solution solution-like born-hidden-knowl\"><summary class=\"knowl__link\"><span class=\"type\">Solution</span></summary><div class=\"solution solution-like knowl__content\"><div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-7-2-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">Pour <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|z| \\to 1\\text{,}\\)</span> le noyau de Poisson <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathcal{P}(t, z)\\)</span> se concentre autour de <span class=\"process-math\">\\(t = \\theta\\text{,}\\)</span> où <span class=\"process-math\">\\(z = r e^{i \\theta}\\text{.}\\)</span> Ainsi, pour <span class=\"process-math\">\\(t\\)</span> loin de <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\theta\\text{,}\\)</span> <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathcal{P}(t, z)\\)</span> devient très petit. En particulier, pour <span class=\"process-math\">\\(t \\in [\\varphi + \\delta, \\varphi + 2\\pi - \\delta]\\text{,}\\)</span> on a :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\mathcal{P}(t, z) \\to 0 \\quad \\text{lorsque } |z| \\to 1\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">Donc l’intégrale sur cet intervalle tend vers 0.</div>\n</div></div></details></div></article><article class=\"exercise exercise-like\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-8\"><h5 class=\"heading\">\n<span class=\"before\"> </span><span class=\"codenumber\">43</span><span class=\"after\"> </span><span class=\"space\"> </span>\n</h5>\n<div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-8-1-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">En utilisant le théorème de Heine, montrer que, pour tout <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\varepsilon \\gt 0\\text{,}\\)</span> il existe <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\delta \\gt 0\\)</span> tel que, pour tout nombre réel <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\varphi\\)</span> et tout nombre complexe <span class=\"process-math\">\\(z\\)</span> vérifiant <span class=\"process-math\">\\(|z| \\lt 1\\text{,}\\)</span>\n</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n|g(z) -\nh(\\varphi)| \\leq \\frac{\\sup_{t \\in \\mathbb{R}} |h(t)|}{\\pi} \\int_{\\varphi +\n\\delta}^{\\varphi + 2\\pi - \\delta} \\mathcal{P}(t, z) \\, dt + \\varepsilon\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n</div>\n<div class=\"solutions\"><details id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-8-2\" class=\"solution solution-like born-hidden-knowl\"><summary class=\"knowl__link\"><span class=\"type\">Solution</span></summary><div class=\"solution solution-like knowl__content\"><div class=\"para logical\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-8-2-1\">\n<div class=\"para\">Par le théorème de Heine, <span class=\"process-math\">\\(h\\)</span> est uniformément continue sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\mathbb{R}\\text{.}\\)</span> Ainsi, pour tout <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\varepsilon \\gt 0\\text{,}\\)</span> il existe <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\delta \\gt 0\\)</span> tel que :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n|t - \\varphi| \\leq \\delta \\implies |h(t) - h(\\varphi)| \\leq \\varepsilon\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">En utilisant cette propriété, on peut écrire :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n|g(z) - h(\\varphi)| \\leq\n\\frac{1}{2\\pi} \\int_{\\varphi - \\delta}^{\\varphi + \\delta} |h(t) - h(\\varphi)|\n\\mathcal{P}(t, z) \\, dt + \\frac{1}{2\\pi} \\int_{\\varphi + \\delta}^{\\varphi + 2\\pi\n- \\delta} |h(t) - h(\\varphi)| \\mathcal{P}(t, z) \\, dt\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">Le premier terme est majoré par <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\varepsilon\\text{,}\\)</span> et le second terme est majoré par :</div>\n<div class=\"displaymath process-math\">\n\\begin{equation*}\n\\frac{\\sup_{t\n\\in \\mathbb{R}} |h(t)|}{\\pi} \\int_{\\varphi + \\delta}^{\\varphi + 2\\pi - \\delta}\n\\mathcal{P}(t, z) \\, dt\n\\end{equation*}\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\">Ainsi, on obtient l’inégalité demandée.</div>\n</div></div></details></div></article><article class=\"exercise exercise-like\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-9\"><h5 class=\"heading\">\n<span class=\"before\"> </span><span class=\"codenumber\">44</span><span class=\"after\"> </span><span class=\"space\"> </span>\n</h5>\n<div class=\"para\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-9-1-1\">Montrer l’existence et l’unicité de la solution au problème de Dirichlet étudié dans cette partie.</div>\n<div class=\"solutions\"><details id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-9-2\" class=\"solution solution-like born-hidden-knowl\"><summary class=\"knowl__link\"><span class=\"type\">Solution</span></summary><div class=\"solution solution-like knowl__content\">\n<div class=\"para\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-9-2-1\">L’existence de la solution est garantie par la construction de la fonction <span class=\"process-math\">\\(g\\text{,}\\)</span> qui est harmonique sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(D(0, 1)\\)</span> et vérifie <span class=\"process-math\">\\(g(\\cos(t), \\sin(t)) = h(t)\\)</span> pour tout <span class=\"process-math\">\\(t \\in \\mathbb{R}\\text{.}\\)</span>\n</div>\n<div class=\"para\" id=\"centrale2018mp2-3-8-9-2-2\">Pour l’unicité, supposons que <span class=\"process-math\">\\(f_1\\)</span> et <span class=\"process-math\">\\(f_2\\)</span> sont deux solutions du problème de Dirichlet. Alors <span class=\"process-math\">\\(f_1 - f_2\\)</span> est harmonique sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(D(0, 1)\\)</span> et nulle sur le bord <span class=\"process-math\">\\(\\partial D(0, 1)\\text{.}\\)</span> Par le principe du maximum, <span class=\"process-math\">\\(f_1\n- f_2 = 0\\)</span> sur <span class=\"process-math\">\\(D(0, 1)\\text{,}\\)</span> donc <span class=\"process-math\">\\(f_1 = f_2\\text{.}\\)</span>\n</div>\n</div></details></div></article></section></section></section></div>\n<div class=\"ptx-content-footer\">\n<a class=\"previous-button
